## Data contracts

Every contract below is derived from the code at 43cd4ff1 (citations) or from rfdetr 1.10.1 (`web-answers.md`). Fields marked **new** do not exist today.

**Seg_Output_Contract (ONNX; Req 4.5, 7.4, 8.1).** Input `input` `[1, 3, R, R]` float32 (`train_rfdetr.py:598-610`). Outputs, static ints **after the exporter's shape stamp** (`stamp_static_output_dims`, §4 / §7: the raw 1.10.1 export declares `masks` with four symbolic dims — spike S2, M-1), graph order as rfdetr sets it (`detr.py:1800-1808` at 1.10.1): `dets [1, Q, 4]` normalised cxcywh; `labels [1, Q, C + 1]` sigmoid logits, slot `C` = background (`export/_onnx/inference.py:148, 263`); `masks [1, Q, R/4, R/4]` raw mask logits (`models/heads/segmentation.py:317-336`, `config.py:515`). `Q` by size: 100/100/200/200/300/300. Identification: shape first, names second, fatal on ambiguity without names. IR ≤ 10 for trained artifacts (`_common.cap_onnx_ir_version`, `_common.py:374-377`); ≤ 9 for converted ones (`FLEET_MAX_IR = 9`, clamped to `TARGET_IR = 8` when exceeded, `export_checkpoint.py:76-80`; re-checked by `detector_conversion.py:1080-1082`). Example: Seg-Nano at 312 → `[1,100,4]`, `[1,100,C+1]`, `[1,100,78,78]`.

**`training_metadata.json` (Seg; Req 4.6, 7.6).** The detection keys (`train_rfdetr.py:622-668`) with these values/additions:

```json
{"detection_arch": "rf_detr_seg", "model_type": "instance_segmentation", "rfdetr_seg_size": "small", "model_class": "RFDETRSegSmall",
 "resolution": 384, "num_classes": 2, "class_names": ["scratch", "dent"], "epochs": 100, "batch": 4, "grad_accum": 4, "lr": 1e-4, "patience": 10,
 "opset": 17, "onnx_opset": 17, "onnx_input_shape": [1, 3, 384, 384],
 "onnx_output_shapes": [[1, 100, 4], [1, 100, 3], [1, 100, 96, 96]], "onnx_output_names": ["dets", "labels", "masks"],
 "logits_slots": 3, "background_slot": 2, "mask_downsample_ratio": 4, "top_k": 100,
 "base_weights": "RFDETRSegSmall" | "<s3 uri>", "base_weights_member": "checkpoint_best_total.pth" (when fine-tuned),
 "metrics": {"test_map50": …, "test_map50_95": …, "test_precision": …, "test_recall": …, "test_mask_map50": …, "test_mask_map50_95": …},
 "manifest_s3": "...", "images_s3": "...",
 "device_manifest_hints": {"layout": "rf_detr", "stage_type": "rf_detr_instance_segmentation", "output_mode": "instance", "network_input": 384,
                           "preserve_aspect": false, "normalize": true, "score_threshold": 0.5, "mask_threshold": 0.5, "top_k": 100}}
```

Conversion adds `source_sha256`, `onnx_sha256`, `exporter`, `torch`, `opset`, `ir_version`, `parity: {<runtime>: {box_max, score_max, slot_agreement, mask_iou_min}}`, `fleet_floor_runtime: "1.16.3"`, `export_image_digest` (the detection keys of `build_conversion_metadata`, `export_checkpoint.py:943`, plus `mask_iou_min`).

**Device manifest (Seg; Req 6.1).** Instance mode (`generate_dda_package` and the shared builder produce this byte-for-byte):

```json
{"runtime": "onnx", "runtime_artifact": "model.onnx",
 "model_graph": {"model_graph_type": "single_stage_model_graph",
   "stages": [{"type": "rf_detr_instance_segmentation", "input_shape": [1,3,384,384], "output_shape": [1,100,3], "output_shapes": [[1,100,4],[1,100,3],[1,100,96,96]],
               "image_width": 384, "image_height": 384, "image_range_scale": true, "normalize": true, "threshold": 0.5, "num_classes": 2}],
   "pixel_level_classes": {"names": ["background", "scratch", "dent"], "normal_ids": [0]}},
 "input_shape": [1,3,384,384], "preprocessing": {"resize": [384, 384], "channel_order": "RGB"},
 "task": "object_detection",
 "detection": {"layout": "rf_detr", "num_classes": 2, "score_threshold": 0.5, "network_input": 384, "preserve_aspect": false, "top_k": 100,
               "mask_threshold": 0.5, "output_mode": "instance", "output_names": ["dets","labels","masks"], "class_names": ["scratch","dent"]},
 "dataset": {"image_width": 384, "image_height": 384}}
```

Semantic mode differs in exactly three places: `stages[0].type = "rf_detr_semantic_segmentation"`, no `task` key, `detection.output_mode = "semantic"`. `output_shape`/`output_shapes` are written by the packager/importer from the real shapes (the builder's nominal `[1, C+4, 8400]` is replaced, as for detection, `packaging.py:497-510`). The D5 component's manifest (stage-level `detection`, no `task`, `pixel_level_classes` with 92 names) is not rewritten; it loads because `setdefault` keeps a stage-level block (`lfv_model_template.py:528-535`) and the decoder defaults the missing keys (§9).

**Triton tensors, instance mode (Req 10.1–10.4).** Unchanged tensor set and dtypes (`lfv_model_template.py:366-372`; `dda_triton/model_convertor.py:656-661`): `output` uint8 `[1]` (1 iff any instance), `output_confidence`/`output_score` float32 `[1]` (top confidence), `mask` = palette RGB `[H, W, 3]` of the Composite_Mask when instances exist and `pixel_level_classes` is non-empty, else zeros of the input shape; `anomalies` = UTF-8 JSON bytes of the Detection_List:

```json
[{"bounding_box": [x_min, y_min, x_max, y_max], "class": "scratch" | "0", "confidence": 0.93, "confidence_threshold": 0.5,
  "class_label": "scratch", "class_index": 0,                                  // class_index NEW (int)
  "mask_rle": "1200 15 1065 17 ...", "mask_area": 4821,                          // NEW; Canonical_RLE string at source resolution
  "mask_truncated": true                                                        // NEW, only when the ceiling dropped this entry's mask_rle
 }, …]                                                                          // descending confidence, ≤ top_k entries
```

Zero instances → the existing sentinel `[{"bounding_box": [], "class": "", "class_label": "", "confidence": 0.0, "no_objects": true}]` (`:343-357`) and an all-zero `mask`. Ceiling: `len(json.dumps(list)) ≤ DETECTION_LIST_MAX_BYTES = 4 MiB` (DD4). YOLO/RF-DETR detection entries carry none of the new keys (byte-identical).

**Capture record, instance mode (Req 10.1, 10.3).** `deviceGroundTruthData[0]`: `source-ref`, `anomaly-label-detected` (1/0), `anomaly-label-detected-metadata {class-name: "Detection", creation-date, human-annotated: "no", type: groundtruth/image-classification, confidence}` as today (`marshal_for_capture_template.py:298-318`), plus — when a mask was written — `anomaly-mask-ref-detected: file://<capture_folder>/<id>.mask.png` and `anomaly-mask-ref-detected-metadata {internal-color-map: {"0": {name: "background", hex-color, total-percentage-area}, "1": {name: <class_label>, …}, …}, creation-date, human-annotated: "no", type: groundtruth/semantic-segmentation, job-name: labeling-job/segmentation-job}` (the anomaly shape, `:319-342`, with DD12's map). `deviceFleetAuxiliaryOutputs`: the `mask.png` and `overlay.jpg` data-refs (`:357-397`), the summary `json` (`{"Inference status", "Inference result": "Detection", "Detection_count", "Confidence", "Anomaly_score", "Anomaly_threshold", "Error msg"}`, `:399-431`), and the label block `json_with_base64_encoding`:

```json
{"detections": {"0": {"class_index": "0", "class_label": "scratch", "bounding_box": [...], "confidence": 0.93,
                      "mask_rle": "...", "mask_area": 4821}, "1": {..., "mask_truncated": true}},
 "detections_truncated": true,                                                   // NEW, only when some entry lost its mask_rle
 "mask_color_map": {"0": {"class-name": "background", "hex-color": "#...", "total-percentage-area": 0.91}, "1": {...}}}   // NEW, only when mask.png written and the palette import succeeded
```

The sidecar `<capture_folder>/<id>.detections.json` carries the same object (`:479-483`). `class_index` stays a **string** in the label block (`str(det.get("class", ""))` today, `:453`; `str(det.get("class_index", det.get("class", "")))` after DD11) — every reader (`inference_results_utils.py:97`, `detections.py:345`, `captures.py:300-344`, HMI) treats it as a string today, so the type is kept. Semantic mode: today's anomaly record exactly (`{"anomalies": {...}}` block, `:494-510`).

**Device DB row (Req 10.5b; DD13).** `inference_result_metadata.detections` JSON, nullable, list form: `[{"class_index": "0", "class_label": "scratch", "bounding_box": [...], "confidence": 0.93, "mask_rle": "...", "mask_area": 4821}, …]` in ascending map-key order; `maskImage` (base64 PNG) and `maskBackground` (`{"class-name", "rgb-color": [r,g,b], "total-percentage-area"}`) populated from `mask.png` and `mask_color_map["0"]`; `anomalyLabels` = the remaining `mask_color_map` entries. `GET /workflows/{id}/results` items and `GET …/results/{capture_id}` carry `detections` (list) unchanged in shape.

**Run preview `/overlay` (Req 10.5c; DD14).** `{"maskImage": <base64|null>, "maskBackground": <dict|null>, "instances": [<list-form entries incl. mask_rle>] | null, "detectionsTruncated": bool}`; the first two keys exactly as today (`run_artifacts.py:234-253`).

**Run metadata `metadata.detections` (Req 10.5d).** `{id, label, confidence, x_min, y_min, x_max, y_max}` (`detections.py:254-290`) plus `mask_area` when present; never `mask_rle`.

**Canonical_RLE (Req 2.2, 3.1, 10.2; DD5).** A space-separated string of run lengths over the **column-major** flattening of a `height × width` binary mask, the first run counting background (`0`) pixels — a leading `0` when the first pixel is foreground (`dda_manifest.py:170-204`). `sum(counts) == width × height`. Frontend parsers accept the string or an int array (`edge-cv-portal/frontend/src/components/labeling/AnnotationCanvas.tsx:134-142`). The COCO writer converts to `{"counts": [ints], "size": [height, width]}` (Req 3.3). Shared vectors: `test/fixtures/canonical_rle_vectors.json` (`{"width", "height", "mask_rows": ["0110", …], "rle": "..."}` entries; the backend file is the source of truth and the frontend test reads the same file through a relative import).

**Labeling annotation, `InstanceSegmentation` (Req 2.2).**

```json
{"modality": "InstanceSegmentation", "image_size": {"width": 1920, "height": 1080},
 "instances": [{"class": "scratch", "rle": "<Canonical_RLE>", "box": {"left": 412, "top": 97, "width": 88, "height": 41}}],
 "classification": "anomaly"}
```

`box` is recomputed server-side from `rle` on submit (DD9); `classification` is derived (`anomaly` iff `instances` non-empty). Storage: the validated annotation is written to S3 (`labeling/<usecase>/<job>/annotations/<task>.json`) and the task item carries `annotation_s3_key`, exactly as Segmentation (`dda_labeling.py:2826-2853`; §2) — never inline, because the RLE payload can exceed DynamoDB's 400 KB item limit.

**Instance_Manifest line (Req 3.1; O8 a).** `source-ref` is the image reference as the writer knows it: the Portal labeling worker writes the image's `s3://` URI (as `_serialize_object_detection` does today); the device export of §11 writes the image **basename** (`os.path.basename(inputImageFilePath)`, the precedent rule of `inference_results_utils.py:378`; images sit beside the manifest in the zip). `_parse_instance_segmentation` accepts any non-empty string and returns it verbatim as `source_ref`; the box fields are always the tight box derived from `rle` (`tight_box_from_rle`, DD9), so a reader may trust them without decoding.

```json
{"source-ref": "s3://<bucket>/<key>.jpg",
 "instance-segmentation": {"image_size": [{"width": 1920, "height": 1080, "depth": 3}],
                           "annotations": [{"class_id": 0, "left": 412, "top": 97, "width": 88, "height": 41, "rle": "<Canonical_RLE>"}]},
 "instance-segmentation-metadata": {"objects": [{"confidence": 1.0}], "class-map": {"0": "scratch", "1": "dent"},
                                    "type": "groundtruth/instance-segmentation", "human-annotated": "yes", "creation-date": "...", "job-name": "labeling-job/<id>"}}
```

`_serialize_instance_segmentation(record, job)` mirrors `_serialize_object_detection` (`dda_manifest.py:463-506`: `class_id` from the Label_Set index, `ValueError` for a box outside the image or a class outside the set, `:475-481`) and additionally raises `ValueError` when `sum(counts) != width × height` or the `rle` has no foreground — both through `tight_box_from_rle(rle, width, height)` (DD9), whose derived box is also what the serializer writes as `left/top/width/height` (so the manifest box can never disagree with the mask, whatever the stored annotation carries). `_parse_instance_segmentation(entry)` is the inverse and is what `parse_manifest` (`:650`) dispatches to for the new modality; `parse_manifest` has no caller at 43cd4ff1 (production or tests), so the serialize → parse round trip in `test_dda_manifest_instance.py` is its first (§11). No `anomaly-mask-ref` key, no PNG (Req 3.1).

**COCO annotation written by the converter (Req 3.3; DD10).** `{"id", "image_id", "category_id": class_id + 1, "bbox": [left, top, w, h], "area": <foreground pixels>, "iscrowd": 0, "segmentation": {"counts": [ints], "size": [height, width]}}`.

**Portal records.** Training record (Req 5.5): `model_type = "instance_segmentation"`, `runtime = "onnx"`, `detection = {detection_arch: "rf_detr_seg", network_input_width, network_input_height, class_names, num_classes, score_threshold, mask_threshold, top_k, preserve_aspect: false, rfdetr_seg_size, resolution, epochs, batch, grad_accum, lr, patience, onnx_opset, sourcedir_s3, base_model: {kind, ref, weights_s3, member, detection_arch, class_names}, output_mode}`; after packaging `packaged_output_mode`. Conversion_Record (Req 7.3): the detection conversion record (`detector_conversion.py:537`) with `model_type = "instance_segmentation"`, `detection.{detection_arch: "rf_detr_seg", rfdetr_seg_size, mask_threshold, top_k, output_mode}`, `metadata.fine_tunable.arch = "rf_detr_seg"`. Imported ONNX record (Req 8.3): `model_type = "instance_segmentation"`, `source = "imported"`, `detection = {detection_arch, num_classes, class_names, score_threshold, mask_threshold, top_k, network_input_width, network_input_height, preserve_aspect: false, output_mode}`.

**Workflow version item (Req 14.8b).** `has_rfdetr_seg_model: bool`, written unconditionally beside `has_stream_features` (`workflow_packaging.py:2931-2947`).

**API request fields (new or extended).** `POST /training`: `model_type: "instance_segmentation"`, `detection_arch: "rf_detr_seg"` (optional, defaulted), `output_mode` (optional, default `instance`), `hyperparameters: {rfdetr_seg_size, resolution, epochs, batch, grad_accum, lr, patience, score_threshold, mask_threshold, onnx_opset}`, `base_model`, `instance_type`. `POST /models/convert`: `model_type: "instance_segmentation"`, `detection_arch` (optional), `output_mode`, `mask_threshold`, `score_threshold`, `class_names` (required for Seg ONNX when the graph has none — always, for ONNX), `num_classes` (optional override), plus the existing fields; the alias `model_type: "segmentation" + detection_arch: "rf_detr"` is accepted and answered with `deprecation`. `POST /models/inspect` response: `suggested_type: "instance_segmentation"`, `detection_arch: "rf_detr_seg"`, `num_classes`, `num_queries`, `mask_grid: [H, W]`, `network_input`, `output_names`, `architecture_hints`, and `disabled_reason` when OFF. `POST /training/{id}/package`: `output_mode` (optional, Seg only). `GET /captures` gains two optional query keys, `key` and `include_masks` (§11; correction C10): a **page** request (no `key`) returns items carrying `detections[].{mask_area, mask_truncated}`, `detections_truncated`, `has_masks`, `mask_url` and `jsonl_key` — never `detections[].mask_rle`, whatever `include_masks` says; a **detail** request (`key=<jsonl_key>&include_masks=1`) returns the same envelope with exactly one capture whose `detections[]` carry `mask_rle`, URLs `None`, `total_found: 1` — the Results_Viewer issues it for the selected capture only; the Portal MCP tool `portal_data_captures_list` can send neither key. Envelope unchanged (`captures`, `bucket`, `prefix`, `total_found`, `expires_in_seconds`, `captures.py:222-228`). Byte bounds: a light page ≈ 3.2 MB for the viewer's 50 × 300-box worst case (≈ 1.9 MB for the MCP tool's 30; ≈ 6.4 MB at the theoretical `limit` 100 worst case, which is **over** the cap and reachable only by a direct API client — the guarantee is scoped to `limit ≤ 50`, §11) and a detail body ≈ 4.2 MB at most (one Detection_List ≤ 4 MiB, DD4) — both under the 6 MB Lambda synchronous response cap (`web-answers.md` WQ-design-review-1); the page is served by `CapturesHandler` at `memorySize: 1024` with the per-record reads on a bounded `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8)` (§11 "Page cost"); `limit` stays ≤ 100 (`:159`), is ignored on a detail request, and `ResultsTab` keeps `limit: 50` unless the Requirement 18.2 measurement forces 20. Device `GET /workflows/{id}/results/export?manifest_format=instance`. All Seg-specific fields are rejected on non-Seg requests with 400 naming the field, so a client cannot send a silent no-op.

**MCP schema (Req 13.1).** `portal_models_convert.inputSchema.properties` += `mask_threshold: number(0,1)`, `output_mode: enum('instance','semantic')`; `detection_arch` enum += `'rf_detr_seg'`; `model_type` enum += `'instance_segmentation'`. `portal_models_training_create.inputSchema.properties` += `output_mode`; the two enums likewise. Edge (`src/backend/mcp_edge/child/catalog.py`, no tool added): `edge_results_list.drop_fields = ('results[].maskImage', 'results[].detections[].mask_rle')`; `edge_execution_overlay_get.drop_fields = ('instances[].mask_rle',)`; `edge_workflow_images_list.drop_fields = ('images[].image', 'images[].inferenceResult.mask_image', 'images[].inferenceResult.detections{}.mask_rle')`; `edge_workflow_run.drop_fields = ('inferenceResult.mask_image', 'inferenceResult.detections{}.mask_rle')`; `edge_result_get.drop_fields = ('detections[].mask_rle',)` (new; `image_field='maskImage'` unchanged — the object-result cap `MAX_RESULT_BYTES = 200_000` makes the drop mandatory, §10). Path grammar (`shaping._PATH_RE`): a segment may end in `[]` (every list element) or, new, `{}` (every value of an object). Portal `__version__ = '1.4.0'`; `portal_data_captures_list` schema and `param_map` unchanged (its handler's `include_masks` default keeps the RLE out, §11).

**Frontend `config.json` (Req 15.1; DD7).** `rfDetrSegEnabled?: boolean` — absent → ON; `false` → hidden.

**CDK context and environment (Req 14.8, 15.1).** Context `enableRfDetrSeg` (absent/true/anything → ON; explicit `false`/`'false'` → OFF). Environment: `PORTAL_RFDETR_SEG_ENABLED: 'true' | 'false'` on `TrainingHandler`, `ModelConverterHandler`, `PackagingHandler`; `WORKFLOW_RFDETR_SEG_MIN_LOCAL_SERVER_VERSIONS: '{"arm64_cpu": "N.N.N", "arm64_jp5": ..., "arm64_jp6": ..., "arm64_jp7": ..., "x86_64": ..., "x86_64_nvidia": ...}'` (empty `{}` pre-enablement) on `DeploymentsHandler` and `WorkflowPackagingHandler`. Output `RfDetrSegEnabled`.

**Gate codes.** `RFDETR_SEG_UNSUPPORTED_ARCH` (packager 409, `{findings: [{code, arch, models}], version, architectures}`); deployments 409 `INCOMPATIBLE_LOCAL_SERVER` details += `rfdetr_seg_model: true`, `rfdetr_seg_min_local_server_versions`.

## Interfaces

New or changed Python/TypeScript signatures, grouped by module. Existing callers keep working: every new parameter has a default that reproduces today's behaviour.


## Error handling

Each row names the condition, where it is detected, whether it is recoverable, what the caller receives, and the log level. "400 naming the field" means the existing `create_response(400, {'error': ...})` text pattern (`training.py:483`, `model_converter.py:1154`).

**Portal request boundary (Lambda; recoverable = the caller fixes the request).**

| Condition | Where | Caller receives | Log |
|---|---|---|---|
| Seg request while `PORTAL_RFDETR_SEG_ENABLED == 'false'` (training, convert incl. alias, inspect of a Seg source reports `disabled_reason` instead, Package `output_mode` override) | handler entry, before body validation | 403 `RF-DETR-Seg is disabled on this portal` (inspect: 200 with `disabled_reason`) | INFO (audit event as for other 403s) |
| `model_type = instance_segmentation` with `detection_arch ∉ {absent, rf_detr_seg}`; or `rf_detr_seg` with another model type | `training.py`, `convert_model`, `validate_conversion_request` | 400 naming both values | none |
| `output_mode ∉ {instance, semantic}`; `output_mode` on a non-Seg request | same | 400 naming the field | none |
| `rfdetr_seg_size` unknown; `resolution` not a positive multiple of the size's block within [240, 1152]; `mask_threshold`/`score_threshold` ∉ (0, 1); other hyperparameter bounds; unknown key | `_parse_rfdetr_seg_hyperparameters` (`ValueError` → 400) | 400 `Invalid hyperparameter '<name>': …` | none |
| `iou_threshold` on a Seg request (`.pth` or ONNX) | `validate_conversion_request`; `convert_model` | 400 `'iou_threshold' does not apply to RF-DETR-Seg` | none |
| Seg `.pth` network input not the size's native resolution (or one of the six when unknown) | `validate_conversion_request` | 400 `RF-DETR-Seg converts at its size's native resolution (<size>: <R>); got <W>` | none |
| `class_names` missing or `len != num_classes` for a Seg ONNX import / `.pth` without names | `convert_model`, `validate_conversion_request` | 400 `class_names must list <num_classes> names` | none |
| `instance_segmentation` ONNX whose graph is not the Seg_Output_Contract | `convert_model` after `inspect_onnx_model` | 400 quoting the output shapes | none |
| `segmentation` ONNX (no alias) whose graph *is* the Seg contract | `convert_model` | 400 `this is an RF-DETR-Seg graph; import it as model_type instance_segmentation` | none |
| Three-output graph ambiguous (`S == 4`, no names) | `inspect_onnx_model` | 200 with `suggested_type: unknown` and a hint; convert → 400 | none |
| Base model arch ≠ `rf_detr_seg` (detection checkpoint for a Seg run, or the reverse); not Completed; other use case; import not fine-tunable | `resolve_base_model` (`ValueError` → 400) | the existing messages with the new arch names | none |
| Manifest offered to Seg training is bbox-only / `anomaly-mask-ref` | `validate_detection_manifest_entry(expected='instance')` (§1) | 400 naming the expected modality; the Manifest Transformer is never suggested | none |
| Instance_Manifest offered to the LFV `classification`/`segmentation` path | `validate_marketplace_manifest` early return before the required-attribute scan (`training.py:222`; §5) | 400 "Manifest is an instance-segmentation manifest; it trains RF-DETR-Seg (model_type instance_segmentation) …", no `transformation_needed`, no Transformer suggestion | none |
| Labeling worker: generated Instance_Manifest fails `_validate_manifest_lines` (missing attribute, non-object pair, `instance-segmentation` pair not detected) | `_validate_manifest_lines` → `ManifestGenerationError` (`dda_labeling_worker.py:1101-1104`) | the job is marked Failed with the issues (`_fail_manifest_generation`, existing path); recoverable by fixing annotations and regenerating | ERROR (existing) |
| Labeling submit: S3 write of the instance annotation fails (`PORTAL_ARTIFACTS_BUCKET` unset or `put_object` error) | the widened Segmentation branch (`dda_labeling.py:2826-2851`) | 500 "Submission could not be saved; the task remains unsubmitted" — the item is not updated, the task stays Assigned (existing Req 7.9 rule of the labeling spec) | ERROR (existing) |
| Edge MCP: object result over `MAX_RESULT_BYTES` after `drop_fields` (an instance row whose boxes alone exceed 200 KB — not reachable at `top_k ≤ 300`: ~150 bytes per entry) | `shaping.bound` (`:334-340`) | `ToolFailure('result_too_large')`, as today for any oversized object result | none (existing) |
| Portal MCP: `portal_data_captures_list` page over the cap | `mcp_portal/shaping` collection cut (`:160-180`) | fewer items with `truncated: true`, as today; the light form (no RLE) keeps a 30-item page far below the cap | none (existing) |
| `GET /captures` **page** over instance captures exceeds API Gateway's 29 s (every selected `.jsonl` is downloaded and decoded whole, `captures.py:214-218, 254, 280-289`; ≈ 5.6 MB per record at the DD4 ceiling) | `list_captures` (§11 "Page cost") | 504 from API Gateway to the viewer while the Lambda (60 s timeout) finishes uselessly; the Results tab shows its existing load error. Mitigated by `memorySize: 1024` on `CapturesHandler` and the bounded `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8)` read; a single unreadable record still degrades to an empty-detections entry (`:253-260`). The page body itself stays under the 6 MB Lambda response cap for `limit ≤ 50`: it is the light form (no `mask_rle`), ≈ 3.2 MB for 50 × 300 boxes (≈ 200 B per entry with the device's float reprs); the theoretical `limit` 100 worst case is ≈ 6.4 MB, over the cap, reachable only by a direct API client, which then lowers `limit`. Recoverable: the Requirement 18.2 run records the measured 50-capture page time (Req 18.5); if > 20 s, `ResultsTab` requests `limit: 20` | WARN per record failure (existing `print`); the page time is recorded in the verification notes |
| `GET /captures` **detail** (`key=<jsonl_key>&include_masks=1`): `key` invalid (not a `str`, empty or > 1024 bytes, outside `search_prefix`, not `.jsonl`) | `list_captures` → `_validate_detail_key` (§11) | 400 `{'error': 'key must be a .jsonl object under the requested prefix'}` after the membership check (a refused caller gets the 403 first, as today); the viewer shows "Masks unavailable for this capture" and keeps boxes. Not recoverable by retry; a Portal client never sends such a key (it echoes `jsonl_key` from the page) | none (client error; the existing `handle_error` path is not reached) |
| `GET /captures` detail: the object is missing (deleted between page and click), unreadable, or its block fails to decode | `_parse_capture` (`:253-260`) | 200 with one degraded entry (`detections: []`, `has_masks: false`, URLs `None`); `ResultsTab` sets `instanceDetailState = 'unavailable'` and the viewer stays on boxes with the warning indicator. Recoverable by reloading the page | WARN per record failure (existing `print`) |
| `GET /captures` detail body over the 6 MB Lambda synchronous response cap | by construction impossible while `DETECTION_LIST_MAX_BYTES = 4 MiB` (DD4: one Detection_List re-serialised ≈ 4.2 MB at most; `tests/test_captures_instances.py` pins `len(body) < 6_000_000` on a 4 MiB fixture) | if the invariant were broken (ceiling raised without redesign), Lambda rejects the response and API Gateway returns 502 — the reason DD4 forbids raising the ceiling; the Req 18.5 note records one measured detail `Content-Length` | n/a (guarded by the unit test and DD4) |
| Labeling submit: instance with empty mask, `rle` inconsistent with `image_size`, class outside the Label_Set, `null` class, polygon with < 3 vertices (client refuses before save) | `_validate_annotation` | 400 with per-field errors naming the instance index | none |
| Labeling job creation: `skip_verification: true` for an `InstanceSegmentation` job | the new check beside the Bedrock-model requirement (`dda_labeling.py:1739-1744`; §2 invariant) | 400 with a `skip_verification` field error ("Skip-verification mode is not available for InstanceSegmentation (no Bedrock pre-labeler supports it)"); the job is not created | none (validation) |
| Package: Seg record with a two-entry `onnx_output_shapes` | `package_trained_detection_component` | 400 `Seg record has a two-output artifact: <shapes>` | ERROR |
| Package: `output_mode` override invalid; override while OFF | `package_components` | 400 / 403 | none / INFO |
| Workflow package: a Seg model and an arch without a floor entry | packager gate | 409 `RFDETR_SEG_UNSUPPORTED_ARCH` with findings; no version item written | WARNING |
| Deploy: device arch without a floor entry or installed version below it | `check_local_server_compatibility` | 409 `INCOMPATIBLE_LOCAL_SERVER` with per-device reasons and the Seg floor map | WARNING |
| MCP call hitting any of the above | backing handler → `shaping.shape` | `ToolFailure` carrying the handler's text (`not_authorized` for 403) | as the handler |

**Training and conversion jobs (SageMaker; fatal = the job fails with a `FATAL:` reason surfaced in Training Detail, as the detection entry points do).**

| Condition | Where | Result | Log |
|---|---|---|---|
| `RESOLUTION` not a multiple of the size's block within [240, 1152]; unknown `RFDETR_SEG_SIZE` | `validate_seg_resolution` / `read_config`, before any download | fatal `FATAL: RESOLUTION must be a multiple of <block> between 240 and 1152 for RF-DETR-Seg <size>` | stdout |
| First manifest line has no instance attribute | the Seg script's own `stage_dataset` → local `first_line_is_instance_manifest` (§4; `det.stage_dataset` is not reused) | fatal naming the problem (bbox-only / anomaly-mask-ref) | stdout |
| `train/` or `valid/` missing or empty after conversion | `assert_splits` | fatal naming the split | stdout |
| An instance's RLE empty or inconsistent with the image size | converter | **recoverable**: instance dropped, `WARN` naming image and instance, `skipped['bad instance rle']` counted; zero-instance image → negative | stdout |
| `--format yolo` on an instance manifest | converter | error exit naming the format (the entry point never requests it) | stderr |
| rfdetr constructor rejects the resolution (backstop) or cannot load weights | `build_model` | fatal `could not load …: <error>` | stdout |
| Training produced no `checkpoint_best_total.pth` | `train_model` | fatal listing the output dir | stdout |
| Export failed / produced no file | `export_onnx` | fatal | stdout |
| Outputs ≠ 3, non-static dims, batch ≠ 1, `Q` disagreement, mask grid ≠ `R/4`, logits slots ≠ `C + 1` (`== C` gets the un-widened-head message), `S == 4` without names | `verify_seg_outputs` | fatal quoting every output | stdout |
| `Q` differs from the size's expected query count | `main` | **recoverable**: `WARN`; metadata `top_k` follows the graph (as `train_rfdetr.py:722-724`) | stdout |
| Mask metric key (`<split>/segm_mAP_50[_95]`) absent from `.evaluate()` result | `seg_metrics_from_eval` | **recoverable**: `NaN` written (precedent rule, `train_rfdetr.py:431-432`; the regex does not match it, so the metric is absent, never a fake 0.0), one `WARN` per missing key | stdout (job log) |
| Conversion: `DETECTION_ARCH` / `TASK` mismatch (`rf_detr_seg` without `TASK=segmentation`, or `TASK=segmentation` on another arch) | `read_config` (C26) | fatal naming both values | stdout |
| Conversion: `NETWORK_INPUT` not a Seg native resolution for `rf_detr_seg` (312 / 384 / 432 / 504 / 624 / 768, or the size's own native when `RFDETR_SIZE` is set) | `read_config` (C26) | fatal naming the allowed inputs | stdout |
| Conversion: `RFDETR_SIZE` not a Seg size for `rf_detr_seg` (or not a detection size for `rf_detr`) | `read_config` (C26) | fatal naming the per-arch table | stdout |
| Conversion: `TASK=segmentation` but no mask head, or the reverse; `RFDETRSegPreview`; keypoint; legacy names | `convert_rfdetr` task gate | fatal via `/opt/ml/output/failure` | stdout |
| Conversion: size shell match ≠ 1; weights did not load key-for-key; requested input ≠ native | `convert_rfdetr` | fatal (existing messages) | stdout |
| Conversion: float16 output, IR/opset above the fleet floor, NaN/Inf on either runtime | `verify_rfdetr_seg_contract`, `run_on_fleet_floor` | fatal | stdout |
| Conversion: box/score parity or `mask_iou_min < 0.99` | `compare_rfdetr_parity` | fatal quoting the maxima/minimum and the pair | stdout |
| Finalize: three-output structure or metadata disagrees with the record — mask shape not `[1, Q, R/4, R/4]` or `Q` differing across outputs; a three-class graph is settled by `output_names` (falling back to output order), never rejected (C30) | `check_onnx_against_record` / `check_metadata_against_record` | record `Failed` naming the rule; 422 to the invoker (precedent) | ERROR |

**Device (never raise into Triton; the capture must complete).**

| Condition | Where | Result | Log |
|---|---|---|---|
| Fewer than three usable tensors, `Q` mismatch, unexpected ranks | `RfDetrSegPostProcessor._split_outputs` | instance mode `[]` → sentinel record; semantic mode all-background `AnomalyResult(0.0)` | WARNING once per processor instance |
| `S == 4` and no `output_names` | `_split_outputs` | list order taken as `[boxes, logits]` | WARNING once |
| `detection.output_mode` contradicts the stage type | `__init__` | stage type wins | ERROR once |
| `num_classes` > available slots | `_select` | slot count used | ERROR once |
| Kept candidate with zero foreground after upsampling | `_select` | dropped (no box without a mask) | DEBUG |
| Detection_List over 4 MiB | base model | `mask_rle` dropped lowest-confidence first, `mask_truncated`/`detections_truncated` set; boxes kept | WARNING per capture (count) |
| `pixel_level_classes` empty on an instance manifest (foreign package) | base model | no composite: empty `mask`, boxes + `mask_rle` still emitted | WARNING once |
| `lyra_anomalies_mask_utils` not importable in the marshal | marshal import | `mask.png` written; `internal-color-map` and `mask_color_map` omitted | WARNING once |
| Malformed `mask_rle` in an entry (overlay fill) | `_fill_instance_masks` | entry skipped in the fill; record unchanged | WARNING |
| Sidecar write fails | `_write_detections_sidecar` | swallowed (existing posture) | WARNING |
| DB save: schema rejects a row (should not happen after DD13) | accessor | existing exception path; the capture file remains for the run preview | ERROR |
| `/overlay` sidecar/record unreadable | `run_artifacts` | `instances: null`, mask as today | WARNING |
| Export: entry without `mask_rle` (truncated) | `generate_instance_manifest` | annotation skipped; export log notes the count | INFO |
| Export: row without a `mask_rle` entry (no instance `detections`, or every entry truncated) | `generate_instance_manifest` | no line for that row; count of skipped rows logged (C24) | INFO |
| HMI/Portal: `mask_rle` fails to decode (length ≠ w × h) | `decodeMaskRle` / `rleToImageData` | that mask skipped; box still drawn; console warning | — |

**Invariants and the layer that owns each.** (1) *Logits carry `C + 1` slots with trailing background* — owned by the trainer's and the conversion job's verifiers (the only places that see the real graph before packaging); the decoder trusts `num_classes`. (2) *A Seg request body is consistent (`model_type` ↔ `detection_arch` ↔ `output_mode` ↔ `iou_threshold` absent)* — owned by the Portal handlers at the request boundary; records and manifests are written only from validated values. (3) *Instance boxes are derived from masks* — owned by `_validate_annotation` on the server (labeling) and by the decoder on the device (boxes come from the model, masks from the model; the export writes both as produced). (4) *Canonical_RLE byte identity across Portal, device, frontend* — owned by the shared vector file and its three tests. (5) *The stage type implies the mode* — owned by `stage_type_for` (the one writer) and the decoder (the one reader). (6) *The Detection_List fits the ceiling* — owned by the base model, the single producer. (7) *No Seg workflow deploys to a pre-Seg LocalServer* — owned by the packager gate and the deployment gate reading one literal. (8) *The Seg_Switch has three readers and no copy elsewhere* — owned by the CDK test on exact Lambda membership.

## Validation of external inputs

Every external input, its rule and the behaviour on failure (the failure column points at the error-handling tables).

| Input | Where | Required | Type and limits | On failure |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `model_type` | `POST /training`, `/models/convert` | yes | one of the existing values + `instance_segmentation` | 400 listing the values |
| `detection_arch` | both | no (defaulted per model type) | `yolo \| rf_detr \| rf_detr_seg`; must agree with `model_type` | 400 naming both |
| `output_mode` | training, convert, package | no (`instance`) | `instance \| semantic`; Seg requests only | 400 naming the field |
| `hyperparameters.rfdetr_seg_size` | training | no (`small`) | one of six | 400 |
| `hyperparameters.resolution` | training | no (native) | int, positive multiple of the size's block, 240 ≤ r ≤ 1152 | 400 with block and bounds |
| `hyperparameters.{epochs, batch, grad_accum, lr, patience, onnx_opset}` | training | no | the RF-DETR bounds (`detection_training.py:362-390`) | 400 |
| `hyperparameters.{score_threshold, mask_threshold}` | training | no (0.5 / 0.5) | float strictly in (0, 1) | 400 |
| unknown hyperparameter key | training | — | rejected (`_reject_unknown`) | 400 |
| `base_model` | training | no (`published`) | `{kind, ref}`; arch of the ref must be `rf_detr_seg`; same use case; Completed | 400 (existing messages) |
| `instance_type` | training | no | existing allow-list | existing 400 |
| manifest first line | training | yes | Instance_Manifest shape for Seg; bbox for detection | 400 naming the expected modality |
| `image_width`/`image_height` (ONNX import) | convert | yes | positive ints; for Seg pre-filled from the graph; must equal the graph input | 400 |
| `num_classes` | convert | no (`S − 1`) | int in [1, S]; `S` allowed for a foreign graph | 400 |
| `class_names` | convert (Seg) | yes | list of non-empty strings, `len == num_classes` | 400 `class_names must list <n> names` |
| `score_threshold`, `mask_threshold` | convert | no (0.5 / 0.5 for Seg) | float in (0, 1) | 400 |
| `iou_threshold` | convert (Seg) | must be absent | — | 400 |
| ONNX graph (Seg) | convert | yes | three static outputs matching the contract; names used for `S == 4` | 400 quoting shapes |
| `.pth` checkpoint | convert | yes | ≤ 512 MiB (`CHECKPOINT_SIZE_CAP`), `rfdetr_checkpoint` kind, Seg task per probe; conversion job re-checks | 400 / job fatal |
| labeling annotation `instances[]` | labeling submit | yes for the modality | each `{class ∈ Label_Set, rle: str}`; `rle` parses to counts summing to `w × h` with at least one foreground run (`tight_box_from_rle`, pure Python, DD9); the client `box` ignored and replaced by the derived one; ≤ 500 instances per image (a sanity cap; the UI never produces more); the validated annotation is stored in **S3** (`annotation_s3_key`), never inline on the DynamoDB item, like Segmentation (`dda_labeling.py:2826`; §2) | 400 per field; 500 on S3 failure (task stays Assigned) |
| `skip_verification` with `task_type == 'InstanceSegmentation'` | labeling job creation (`POST` DDA labeling job, beside `dda_labeling.py:1739-1744`) | — | boolean; `true` is not allowed for the new modality (no Bedrock pre-labeler supports it; §2 invariant) | 400 `skip_verification` field error; the three existing modalities keep today's rule |
| `include_masks` | `GET /captures` query | no (`false`) | `'1'`/`'true'` → true; anything else false; honoured only together with `key` (ignored on a page request, so a page never carries `mask_rle`) | never fails (treated as false) |
| `key` | `GET /captures` query (detail request) | no (absent = page request) | `str`, 1–1024 bytes, `startswith(search_prefix)` (prefix scoped with `device_id` exactly as the page would list, `captures.py:189-191`), `endswith('.jsonl')`; validated after `check_user_access` (`:170-172`) and before the credential resolution (`:175-178`); `limit` ignored when present | 400 `key must be a .jsonl object under the requested prefix` |
| `manifest_format` | device export | no (`classification`) | `classification \| instance` | 400 (FastAPI `RequestValidationError`, answered 400 by the device app's validation handler, `exception_handlers.py:92` — the `captureIdPath` precedent; M-7) |
| `WORKFLOW_RFDETR_SEG_MIN_LOCAL_SERVER_VERSIONS` | Lambda env | no (`{}`) | JSON object arch → `N.N.N` (`_parse_min_versions_map`) | malformed → treated as empty (fail closed) + ERROR log, as the stream floor |
| `PORTAL_RFDETR_SEG_ENABLED` | Lambda env | no | `'true' \| 'false'` (anything but `'false'` is ON) | — |
| `config.json.rfDetrSegEnabled` | frontend | no | boolean; only `false` hides | — |
| device manifest `detection.{output_mode, top_k, output_names, mask_threshold}` | decoder | no | mode must match the stage type; `top_k` int > 0; names list of 3 | logged, defaults applied (device never fails on manifest fields) |
| `mask_rle` from the device (Portal/HMI) | viewers | — | counts sum to `w × h` | mask skipped, box drawn |

## Testing strategy

Commands follow the precedent specs (`detector-checkpoint-import/tasks.md:13-16`): backend from `edge-cv-portal/backend` with `~/.venvs/dda-portal-tests/bin/python -m pytest tests/<file> -q -p no:cacheprovider`, targeted files only; device suites from the repo root with `python3 -m pytest test/backend-test/<file> -q -p no:cacheprovider` (the `test/backend-test/conftest.py` fixtures); Portal frontend (`edge-cv-portal/frontend`, vitest ^4.1.11 in `package.json`): `npx vitest run <file>` then `npm run build`; HMI (`src/frontend`, CRA/Jest — `package.json` `"test": "react-scripts test"`, `react-scripts 5.0.1`, no vitest dependency, 37 Jest test files): `CI=true npm test -- --watchAll=false --testPathPattern=<pattern>` then `npm run build` (the command of the precedent that created the HMI suite, `.kiro/specs/edge-workflow-run-ux/tasks.md:7, 77`); HMI tests use Jest globals and `@testing-library/react`, placed as `*.test.ts(x)` beside the component; CDK `npm test -- <file>` in `infrastructure/`; the two drift guards and the full preservation suite per `.kiro/steering/builds.md`. Every criterion of Requirements 1–16 that names a test maps to a file below; the pins that move are listed last.

**Unit-testable (pure, no AWS, no device).**
- `tests/test_detection_training_shared.py`: `test_detection_arch_vocabulary` extended (never relaxed) to the three-entry tuple and the `rf_detr_seg` map entries (Req 1.1, 17.1); the arch-parametrised cases at `:612` (`test_job_environment_base_weights_only_when_passed`) and `:943` (`test_resolve_base_model_published`) pass without edit; the entry-point-parametrised `test_build_sourcedir_tarball_tolerates_missing_common` (`:718-726`) **moves** (see "Pins that move": fixture `_CODE_DIR_FILES` `:647-655` gains `train_rfdetr_seg.py`, the exact member set gains `_SIBLINGS_FOR_ENTRY_POINT[entry_point]`); new cases for `_parse_rfdetr_seg_hyperparameters` (six natives accepted, 320 rejected everywhere, 324 accepted for nano only, bounds, `mask_threshold`, unknown key), `detection_job_environment` for Seg (`RFDETR_SEG_SIZE`, no thresholds, `BASE_WEIGHTS_*` only when passed), `stage_type_for`, `is_detr_family`, `rfdetr_seg_enabled` (absent/true/false), `validate_detection_manifest_entry(expected='instance')` both rejections, `build_sourcedir_tarball` members per arch (Seg includes `train_rfdetr.py`), `resolve_base_model` Seg matrix (published / training_job / imported, arch mismatch both ways), `METRIC_DEFINITIONS_FOR_ARCH` identity for yolo/rf_detr.

**Gates before build/deploy (Req 17.6, 17.7).** Backend targeted files green; device suites green; `npx vitest run` + `npm run build` in `edge-cv-portal/frontend`; `CI=true npm test -- --watchAll=false` + `npm run build` in `src/frontend` (Jest; see Commands); `npm run build` + `npm test` in `infrastructure/`; `iam_audit`, `repo_audit`, secrets audit green; the drift guards with `cdk.out` moved aside; the full preservation suite at or better than baseline; ComputeStack template size recorded. Then the six LocalServer builds (one at a time, no portal deploy during a build), the Portal deploy **flag-less** — an explicit `-c deployGroundedSamWorker=false` is the deliberate teardown path and deletes the live Grounded-SAM worker (`DdaGroundedSamWorker`, `compute-stack.ts:3141-3160`; `portal-deploy-flag-hardening`, `detector-checkpoint-import/tasks.md:21`), which Requirement 2.4 pre-labels and the 18.2(a) labeling run need; the worker image itself is not changed by this spec (Req 2.4 reuses its contract), and the `DdaLabelingWorker` **Lambda** (`:2837`), which does change, deploys with the ComputeStack like every other handler (C13) — then the floor entries in one change, redeploy, Req 18.

## Spike (task 0): questions the code cannot settle

The precedent specs opened with an exploration spike whose answers were recorded in `docs/` (`transfer-learning-spike.md`, `detector-checkpoint-import-spike.md`). This spec needs one too, because four facts (S4–S7) cannot be settled from the repository or the vendor source (no rfdetr install on this host; `web-answers.md` read the source but ran nothing), and three source-settled facts (S1–S3) deserve a live confirmation before the probe, verifier and metric mapping are trusted. The spike runs in the Export_Image (a Seg export + conversion) and as one short SageMaker job (Seg-Nano, a two-class Instance_Manifest of ~40 images, 2 epochs). Its deliverable is `docs/rfdetr-seg-spike.md` with the answers below, each with the command and the observed value; tasks that depend on an answer are marked in tasks.md and may not start before it is recorded. Design defaults hold unless the spike contradicts them, in which case the design is amended in the same commit as the spike doc.

| # | Question | Design default (used until answered) | What settles it | Depends |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | Seg mask-head state-dict prefix, the `args.segmentation_head` flag and the optional `model_name` — **settled at source** (`WQ-design-author-1`: prefix `segmentation_head.` from `lwdetr.py:155`; `args.segmentation_head` read by `utilities/state_dict.py:375-395`; `model_name` written by PTL checkpoints since 1.7.0, absent from `export_for_roboflow` files). The spike only confirms on a live artifact that `classify_checkpoint` labels the published Seg-Nano `.pth` `rfdetr_checkpoint` with `rfdetr_task = 'segmentation'`, `num_classes = 90` and the expected `args_picks` | as designed in §7 | download the published Seg-Nano `.pth` inside the image; run the probe; diff the evidence against the fixture builder's | Req 7.1; §7 probe; fixture builder |
| S2 | Output names and static shapes of a 1.10.1 Seg export **as loaded by the Export_Image's own onnxruntime (1.30.0 at 43cd4ff1, `requirements.lock:38`), the Fleet_Floor_Runtime 1.16.3 (`ort-floor.lock:10`) and the trainer DLC's 1.19.2 (`requirements-rfdetr.txt:8`, the pin behind `read_onnx_io` / `verify_seg_outputs`)** (C17) (`dets`/`labels`/`masks`, `[1,Q,4]`, `[1,Q,91]`, `[1,Q,R/4,R/4]`) — the names-as-set and static-dims-by-construction half is **settled at source** (`WQ-req-author-3`), and the reference's return shape is **settled at source** (`WQ-design-author-3`: `forward_export` returns the tuple `(pred_boxes, pred_logits, pred_masks)`, DD17); the spike confirms the numerics only (what the two runtimes report after `onnxsim`) | names present, dims static; `out[:3]` | `trainer.export_onnx` on the published Seg-Nano; `onnx.load` + the two image runtimes (spike run (i)) and the DLC runtime after `cap_onnx_ir_version` (spike run (ii)); one `reference(batch)` call | Req 4.5, 7.4; §4 verifier; §7 reference |
| S3 | The mask metric keys rfdetr's `.evaluate()` returns for a seg model — **settled at source** (`WQ-design-author-2`: flat `"<split>/<metric>"` keys; the only mask keys are `test/segm_mAP_50_95` and `test/segm_mAP_50`, `coco_eval.py:128, 870-873`); the spike confirms the two keys appear in a real 2-epoch run | `_SEG_EVAL_KEY_MAP` of §4 | the 2-epoch job's `rfdetr evaluate(test): {...}` log line | Req 4.7 |
| S4 | Mask-IoU parity between the torch reference and the ONNX export on both runtimes for the bundled image (`PARITY_TOLERANCE['rf_detr_seg']['mask_iou']`) | 0.99 | `compare_rfdetr_parity` in the image | Req 7.5 |
| S5 | Whether `rfdetr[train,onnx]==1.10.1` installs everything the seg trainer and exporter import (no missing extra) and whether the Export_Image needs a pin change | pins unchanged | the job's pip install log; `import` smoke in the image | Req 4.1, 7.8, 14.4 |
| S6 | Decode cost on a Jetson-class CPU: per-instance `cv2.resize` to 1920×1080 + threshold + `packbits`, `unpackbits` + paint, RLE encode (DD3/DD5), at 10 and 100 instances | recorded in §24 of the device doc | the device suite's timing fixture on the JP6 verification device (or the x86 sandbox, labelled) | Req 10.3, 16.1 |
| S7 | Whether the published Seg weights for all six sizes download through rfdetr's resolver on the DLC (XL/2XL were never fetched in DDA). **If a size's weights do not resolve:** the size stays in every table (D4 is an owner decision), a training run of that size fails with the existing "could not load … " base-weights fatal (`build_model`, §4) naming the size, the spike document records the size and the exact error for the owner, and the trainer README lists the size as "published weights unavailable at <date>" until it resolves | all six available | `RFDETRSeg<Size>()` construction for each size in the job | D4, Req 4.3 |

Only S4–S7 are genuinely open; S1–S3 are confirmations of source-settled facts. The contingencies written here before the spike were: if S2 shows a symbolic query dim in any output, `verify_seg_outputs` additionally accepts a `dim_param` equal across the three outputs and records it as `Q` with `onnx_output_shapes` carrying the static `Q` from the size table; if S5 shows a missing extra, `requirements-rfdetr.txt` and the Export_Image lock are amended with the reason recorded (Req 4.1, 7.8). **Outcome (task 0.3, 2026-11-22; `docs/rfdetr-seg-spike.md`, run records' `mismatches.md` M-1 … M-3).** S1: confirmed with amendment M-2 — the published Seg-Nano `.pth` is `rfdetr_checkpoint` / `num_classes 90` with 35 `segmentation_head.*` keys but a stripped `{"model"}`-only envelope (no `args`, `model_name`), and a 1.10.1 training checkpoint's `args` is the TrainConfig dump (model fields only in the EMA file's `model_config`); §7 "Probe" amended. S2: names confirmed in all three runtimes; `dets` / `labels` static, **`masks` declared with four symbolic dims in the protobuf** (static `[1, 100, 78, 78]` at run time; 1.30.0 resolves it in session metadata, 1.19.2 and 1.16.3 do not; `Q` itself is static, so the `dim_param` contingency is **not** taken) — remedy M-1: one post-export `stamp_static_output_dims` in the trainer (§4) and the conversion job (§7), a tolerant branch for foreign unstamped exports in the inspector (§8), the glossary and Req 4.5 reworded; IR stamped 8 (`cap_onnx_ir_version` → `(8, 8)`, kept as the ceiling); **1.16.3 loads and runs the graph** — the stop rule is not triggered, the O11 a coverage claim for JP5 / x86_64 (CPU and Nvidia) / arm64_cpu stands; 1.19.2 opens the capped graph, so `read_onnx_io` stays a runtime load (no `shape_inference` fallback, no README note, no trainer-pin question). S3: both mask keys present (`test/segm_mAP_50`, `test/segm_mAP_50_95`); `_SEG_EVAL_KEY_MAP` unchanged. S4: mask IoU min 1.0 on both image runtimes — `PARITY_TOLERANCE['rf_detr_seg']['mask_iou'] = 0.99` holds (§7 / DD17 unchanged). S5: no missing extra; one missing **pin** — M-3 / OR-1: `requirements-rfdetr.txt` gains `transformers==5.17.0` (the trainer DLC resolved 5.24.0 and `import rfdetr` failed on `BackboneConfigMixin`; also repairs the detection trainer), the Export_Image lock already pins it (no 17.1 change). S6: x86 figures recorded for §24 (49 ms / 447 ms at 10 / 100 instances, labelled). S7: all six sizes resolve in both environments — no README caveat. The `encoder` question raised during the design (WQ-design-author-4) was settled at source (every Seg config inherits `dinov2_windowed_small`, `config.py:772`) and is not a spike item.

## Requirements corrections for tasks.md

The requirements were approved on pass 5 with four non-blocking NITs (`requirements-review.md`, pass 5). This design resolves each; where the resolution needs a sentence in `requirements.md`, the tasks author carries it as task 0.x (the committed requirements document is not edited by this step). C8–C12 come from the design review loop (pass 1 finding 5; pass 2 findings 5a and 5b; pass 3 findings 1 and 2): each is a code fact (`shaping.resolve_path` list-only fan-out; the two MCP servers' 200 000-byte result caps; the HMI's Jest runner; the entry-point-parametrised tarball pin) that the requirements text did not account for. None re-opens D1–D5 or O1–O16 — O2 b fixes the mask *encoding*, not which HTTP responses carry it by default. **Applied:** task 0.1 (2026-11-22) applied C1–C12 of this table to `requirements.md`, the tasks-review corrections C13–C34 and the five pass-5 NITs of this document (recorded in tasks.md 0.1) to both documents, and the base-drift corrections BD1–BD9 (tasks.md Notes, "Base drift": the branch is rebased onto `origin/main` `aa6d912f`; code stays cited at 43cd4ff1 with the line shifts recorded there) — one commit, before any code task.

| # | Source | Resolution in this design | Requirements text change for task 0.x |
|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | Pass 5 NIT 1 — 14.8(d)(i) enters a per-arch candidate "as soon as its build is published", but the coverage test (e) accepts only an absent/empty literal before full coverage | §14 "Enablement order": the six entries are made in **one change** after all six criterion-5 builds exist; a late arch is listed in `UNVERIFIED_RFDETR_SEG_ARCHES` with its reason until its build lands; JP7/JP6 candidates are the values entered in that change, raised by a fixed build | After 14.8(d)(iv): "The six entries SHALL be made in one change after all six criterion-5 builds of 14.5 exist (the coverage test (e) accepts an empty literal but, once non-empty, requires every arch outside the unverified set); an arch whose criterion-5 build is not published in the window is handled as in (iii) — listed in the unverified set with the reason — until its build exists." In (i) replace "WHEN the first criterion-5 build of an arch is published" with "WHEN the six criterion-5 builds are published (or, for a late arch, when its build lands and the unverified entry is removed)". |

### Pass 4 (`design-review.md` pass 4, 2026-11-21: 1 HIGH, 0 MEDIUM, 4 NIT; verdict CHANGES_REQUESTED)

Every finding was re-verified against the worktree at 43cd4ff1 before the fix (`captures.py:137-231, 234-277, 300-344, 374-390`, `api-gateway-stack.ts:194-199, 209-212, 402-411`, `auth_middleware.py:248-271`, `marshal_for_capture_template.py:161, 172, 193, 241-268, 269, 453, 480-492, 532, 548, 551`, `ResultsTab.tsx:1-64, 120-175`, `ResultsViewer.tsx:50-75`, `api.ts:2070-2093`, `usecases.py:558-606`, `compute-stack.ts:2157`, `com.dda.InferenceUploader/artifacts/inference_uploader.py:36-77, 189-191`, `tests/test_captures.py:128-156, 196-502`); the AWS quotas come from `web-answers.md` WQ-design-review-1. All five findings are addressed, none backlogged, none ignored. No owner decision is touched: finding 1 changes which HTTP request carries the Canonical_RLE to the Portal viewer (one capture per request instead of the page), not the O2 b encoding, the O1 a device contract, the DD4 ceiling or the C10 light default for the Portal MCP tool; D1–D5 and O1–O16 stand as recorded.

1. **HIGH — `GET /captures?limit=50&include_masks=1` returned every `mask_rle` on the page through a buffered Lambda proxy integration (15–50 MB at the design's own sizes; Lambda synchronous response cap 6 MB, API Gateway 10 MB).** Fixed with the reviewer's option (a), per-capture fetch on the same route. §11 `captures.py` now defines two request shapes on the one `GET /captures` resource (no new route, Req 14.3): the **page** (no `key`) is always the light form — boxes, `has_masks`, `mask_area`, `mask_truncated`, `detections_truncated`, `mask_url` and the new `jsonl_key` — and never carries `mask_rle`, whatever `include_masks` says (bound ≈ 3.2 MB for the viewer's 50 × 300 boxes at ≈ 200 B per entry; the theoretical `limit` 100 worst case is ≈ 6.4 MB, over the cap, so the guarantee is scoped to `limit ≤ 50` — pass 5 NIT 2); the **detail** (`key=<jsonl_key>&include_masks=1`) validates `key` (`str`, 1–1024 bytes, `startswith(search_prefix)`, `endswith('.jsonl')`, else 400) after the membership check, skips the paginator and runs `_parse_capture` once with `all_keys={key}` (one `get_object` with the owner assertion, URLs `None`, page envelope, `total_found: 1`). DD4 now states that the 4 MiB ceiling also bounds this response (one Detection_List re-serialised ≈ 4.2 MB at most, both sides `json.dumps` with default separators, `marshal_for_capture_template.py:485`, `auth_middleware.py:271`) and names both caps with the integration type (`api-gateway-stack.ts:199, 211, 404-410`), so `DETECTION_LIST_MAX_BYTES` may not be raised without redesigning the path; `tests/test_captures_instances.py` pins `len(response['body']) < 6_000_000` on a 4 MiB fixture and the page's RLE-free shape. `ResultsTab` keeps its page call unchanged and issues the detail from a `useEffect` for the selected capture only (memoised per `capture_id`), passing `instanceDetections`/`instanceDetailState` to the prop-driven `ResultsViewer` (spinner while loading, "Masks unavailable" warning on a degraded detail); `api.ts getCaptures` gains `key`/`include_masks`. Updated in the same change: Overview diagram, Data contracts "API request fields", Interfaces (`_validate_detail_key`, `_parse_capture`, `getCaptures`, the three viewer types), Validation (`key`; `include_masks` honoured only with `key`), Error handling (the page 504 row gains the body bound; three new detail rows: 400, degraded 200, the by-construction cap), Testing strategy (backend cases, new `ResultsTab.test.tsx`, `ResultsViewer.instances.test.tsx` prop-driven), correction C10 ("for the selected capture only"; 11.5 and 18.5 appendices), Traceability rows 11 and 18, and "Page cost" (the Req 18.5 measurement now records the light-page time and one detail request's time and `Content-Length`; a new contingency lowers the ceiling if a detail body ever measures above 5 MB). Option (b), a presigned `<id>.detections.json` `detections_url`, is recorded as rejected with the reason from code: a browser `fetch()` needs a CORS GET rule on `dda-inference-results-*`, and the repository's bucket-CORS writers (`usecases.py:558`, `s3_cors.ensure_bucket_cors` at `layers/shared/python/s3_cors.py:17` via `data_management.py:597, 681` and `model_converter.py:349`, and `compute-stack.ts:2157`) target the Data Account, use-case and component buckets, never `dda-inference-results-*` (design review pass 5 NIT 1) — the results bucket is derived and asserted, never created or configured, by Portal or device code — and the sidecar is best-effort (`marshal_for_capture_template.py:241-268`).
2. **NIT — `has_masks` false for an all-truncated capture.** Fixed: §11 defines `has_masks = any("mask_rle" in d or d.get("mask_truncated") for d in block["detections"].values())`, states that the viewer shows the truncated-instances note whenever `detections_truncated` is set (toggle or detail present or not — the page's light entries already carry `mask_truncated`), and the all-truncated record joins `tests/test_captures_instances.py` (both the `list_captures` page case and the `_capture_mask_flags` cases); C10's requirements text now spells the rule ("`mask_rle` present or `mask_truncated` set").
3. **NIT — `rfdetr_seg_enabled() -> bool` listed twice.** Fixed: the Interfaces `detection_training.py` block keeps one line, `# DD7: env PORTAL_RFDETR_SEG_ENABLED, absent → True (listed once)`; the duplicate last line is removed.
4. **NIT — device RLE module function names.** Fixed: `utils/mask_rle.py` exposes exactly `encode(mask) -> str` and `decode(rle, width, height)`; DD5 says so and uses `decode`, §9's `deserialize` comment reads `mask_rle.decode`, the Interfaces block already had the two names. The Portal-side `_rle_counts`/`rle_decode` mentions in the `dda_manifest.py` Interfaces block refer to the shared layer's existing `rle_decode` (`dda_manifest.py:205-245`) and are unchanged on purpose.
5. **NIT — five line citations.** Fixed: §11 "Today" cites `_extract_inference_data` as `captures.py:300-344` (and adds `list_captures :137-231`, `_parse_capture :234-277` with their inner ranges), and the `captures.py` paragraph's `:300-346` now reads `:300-344`; §10 "Today" cites `_generate_capture_meta_data` (`:269-526`), `_has_anomaly_mask` (`:548-549`) and `execute` (`:551-660`); DD11 and the Data-contracts "Capture record" paragraph cite the label-block `class_index = str(det.get("class", ""))` at `marshal_for_capture_template.py:453` (the `:193` twin belongs to `_generate_detection_overlay` and is not cited for the label block).
